&&\left.\left.-\,\frac{I_{q}}{I^{\dagger}_{q}}\frac{S_{i}}{S^{\dagger}_{i}}\frac{E^{\dagger}_{i}}{E_{i}}\right)+\bar{u}_{i}\left(2\ell+6-\frac{S^{\dagger}_{i}}{S_{i}}-\frac{I_{i}}{I^{\dagger}_{i}}\frac{R^{\dagger}_{i}}{R_{i}}-\frac{S^{\dagger}_{q}}{S_{q}}-\;\frac{R_{i}}{R^{\dagger}_{i}}\frac{S_{q}}{S^{\dagger}_{q}}\frac{E^{(1)\dagger}_{r}}{E^{(1)}_{r}}-{\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{l}}\frac{E^{(j)}_{q}}{E^{(j)\dagger}_{q}}\frac{E^{(j+1)\dagger}_{r}}{E^{(j+1)}_{r}}-\,{\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{l}}\frac{E^{(j)}_{r}}{E^{(j)\dagger}_{r}}\frac{E^{(j)\dagger}_{q}}{E^{(j)}_{q}}-\frac{E^{(l+1)}_{r}}{E^{(\ell+1)\dagger}_{r}}\frac{I^{\dagger}_{q}}{I_{q}}-\frac{I_{q}}{I^{\dagger}_{q}}\frac{S_{i}}{S^{\dagger}_{i}}\frac{I^{\dagger}_{i}}{I_{i}}\right)\right)\\
&&+\,{\displaystyle\sum_{i\in\mathcal{E}}}\varpi_{i}\widetilde{\nu}_{i}E^{\dagger}_{i}\left(1+\frac{I_{q}}{I^{\dagger}_{q}}-\frac{S^{\dagger}_{i}}{S_{i}}\frac{E_{i}}{E^{\dagger}_{i}}-\frac{I_{q}}{I^{\dagger}_{q}}\frac{S_{i}}{S^{\dagger}_{i}}\frac{E^{\dagger}_{i}}{E_{i}}\right)\\
&&+\,{\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{n}}\varpi_{i}(\bar{v}_{i}\gamma_{i}+\widetilde{\nu}_{i})I^{\dagger}_{i}\left(u_{i}\left(2+\frac{I_{q}}{I^{\dagger}_{q}}-\frac{S^{\dagger}_{i}}{S_{i}}\frac{I_{i}}{I^{\dagger}_{i}}-\frac{I^{\dagger}_{i}}{I_{i}}\frac{E_{i}}{E^{\dagger}_{i}}-\frac{I_{q}}{I_{q}^{\dagger}}\frac{S_{i}}{S^{\dagger}_{i}}\frac{E^{\dagger}_{i}}{E_{i}}\right)+\bar{u}_{i}\left(1+\frac{I_{q}}{I^{\dagger}_{q}}-\frac{S^{\dagger}_{i}}{S_{i}}\frac{I_{i}}{I^{\dagger}_{i}}-\,\frac{I_{q}}{I_{q}^{\dagger}}\frac{S_{i}}{S^{\dagger}_{i}}\frac{I^{\dagger}_{i}}{I_{i}}\right)\right)\\
&&+\,{\displaystyle\sum_{i\in\mathcal{R}}}\varpi_{i}\left(\zeta_{i}+\widetilde{\nu}_{i}\right)R^{\dagger}_{i}\left(u_{i}\left(3+\frac{I_{q}}{I^{\dagger}_{q}}-\frac{I^{\dagger}_{i}}{I_{i}}\frac{E_{i}}{E^{\dagger}_{i}}-\frac{I_{i}}{I^{\dagger}_{i}}\frac{R^{\dagger}_{i}}{R_{i}}-\frac{R_{i}}{R^{\dagger}_{i}}\frac{S^{\dagger}_{i}}{S_{i}}-\frac{I_{q}}{I_{q}^{\dagger}}\frac{S_{i}}{S^{\dagger}_{i}}\frac{E^{\dagger}_{i}}{E_{i}}\right)+\bar{u}_{i}\left(2+\frac{I_{q}}{I^{\dagger}_{q}}-\frac{I_{i}}{I^{\dagger}_{i}}\frac{R^{\dagger}_{i}}{R_{i}}-\frac{R_{i}}{R^{\dagger}_{i}}\frac{S^{\dagger}_{i}}{S_{i}}-\frac{I_{q}}{I_{q}^{\dagger}}\frac{S_{i}}{S^{\dagger}_{i}}\frac{I^{\dagger}_{i}}{I_{i}}\right)\right).\end{array}

which together with (
73
) yields expression (
38
).

Appendix C
Nonstandard finite difference scheme

The Nonstandard finite difference scheme uses for the simulations is:

\left\{\begin{aligned} \frac{S^{p+1}_{i}-S^{p}_{i}}{\phi(\delta t)}&~~=~~\Lambda_{i}+\widetilde{\nu_{i}}H^{p}_{i}-\nu_{i}S^{p}_{i}-a\,\varphi_{i}\frac{I^{p}_{q}}{H^{p}}S^{p+1}_{i}+\bar{v}_{i}\gamma_{i}I^{p}_{i}+v_{i}\zeta_{i}R^{p}_{i}&i=0,\;1,\;\cdots,\;n\\
\frac{S^{p+1}_{q}-S^{p}_{q}}{\phi(\delta t)}&~~=~~\Gamma-\mu S^{p}_{q}-(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})S^{p+1}_{q}+\delta S^{p}_{r}&\,\\
\frac{S^{p+1}_{r}-S^{p}_{r}}{\phi(\delta t)}&~~=~~(r_{suc}^{p}-r_{inf}^{p})S^{p+1}_{q}-(\mu+\delta)S^{p}_{r}&\,\\
\frac{E^{(1)p+1}_{r}-E^{(1)p}_{r}}{\phi(\delta t)}&~~=~~r_{inf}^{p}S^{p+1}_{q}-(\mu+\delta)E^{(1)p}_{r}&\,\\
\frac{E^{p+1}_{i}-E^{p}_{i}}{\phi(\delta t)}&~~=~~u_{i}a\,\varphi_{i}\frac{I^{p}_{q}}{H^{p}}S^{p+1}_{i}-u_{i}\left(\varepsilon_{i}+\nu_{i}\right)E^{p}_{i}&i=0,\;1,\;\cdots,\;n\,\\
\frac{E^{(j)p+1}_{q}-E^{(j)p}_{q}}{\phi(\delta t)}&~~=~~\delta E^{(j)p}_{r}-\mu E^{(j)p}_{q}-(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})E^{(j)p+1}_{q}&j=1,\;2,\;\cdots,\;l\,\\
\frac{E^{(j+1)p+1}_{r}-E^{(j+1)p}_{r}}{\phi(\delta t)}&~~=~~r_{suc}^{p}E^{(j)p+1}_{q}-(\mu+\delta)E^{(i+1)p}_{r}&j=1,\;2,\;\cdots,\;l\,\\
\frac{I^{p+1}_{i}-I^{p}_{i}}{\phi(\delta t)}&~~=~~\bar{u}_{i}a\,\varphi_{i}\frac{I^{p}_{q}}{H^{p}}S^{p+1}_{i}+u_{i}\varepsilon_{i}E^{p}_{i}-\left(\gamma_{i}+\nu_{i}\right)I^{p}_{i}&i=0,\;1,\;\cdots,\;n\,\\
\frac{R^{p+1}_{i}-R^{p}_{i}}{\phi(\delta t)}&~~=~~v_{i}\gamma_{i}I^{p}_{i}-v_{i}\left(\zeta_{i}+\nu_{i}\right)R^{p}_{i}&i=0,\;1,\;\cdots,\;n\,\\
\frac{I^{p+1}_{q}-I^{p}_{q}}{\phi(\delta t)}&~~=~~\delta E^{(l+1)p}_{r}-\mu I^{p}_{q}-(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})I^{p+1}_{q}+\delta I^{p}_{r}&\,\\
\frac{I^{p+1}_{r}-I^{p}_{r}}{\phi(\delta t)}&~~=~~r_{suc}^{p}I^{p+1}_{q}-(\delta+\mu)I^{p}_{r}\end{aligned}\right.

(76)

where

r_{inf}^{p}=\frac{a}{H^{p}}{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}}f_{i}c_{i}\bar{k}_{i}I^{p}_{i};\quad r_{suc}^{p}=\frac{a}{H^{p}}{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}}f_{i}c_{i}\bar{k}_{i}H^{p}_{i};\quad d^{p}=\frac{a}{H^{p}}{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}}k_{i}H^{p}_{i}.

The time step function
\phi(t)
is defined as

\phi(t)=\dfrac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-th}}{h},\textrm{~~with~}h=\max(\delta+\mu,\;\nu_{1}+\gamma_{1},\;\cdots,\;\nu_{n}+\gamma_{n},\;u_{1}(\varepsilon_{1}+\nu_{1}),\;\cdots,\;u_{n}(\varepsilon_{n}+\nu_{n}),\;v_{1}(\zeta_{1}+\nu_{1}),\;\cdots,\;v_{n}(\zeta_{n}+\nu_{n})).

Solving (
76
) for the
p+1^{th}
terms give the
following semi-implicit system of difference equations:

\left\{\begin{aligned} S^{p+1}_{i}&~=~\frac{\phi(\delta t)\Lambda_{i}}{1+\phi(\delta t)a\varphi_{i}\frac{I^{p}_{q}}{H^{p}}}+\frac{1-\phi(\delta t)\left(\nu_{i}-\widetilde{\nu}_{i}\right)}{1+\phi(\delta t)a\varphi_{i}\frac{I^{p}_{q}}{H^{p}}}S^{p}_{i}+\frac{\phi(\delta t)\widetilde{\nu}_{i}}{1+\phi(\delta t)a\varphi_{i}\frac{I^{p}_{q}}{H^{p}}}E^{p}_{i}\\
&~~~+~\frac{\phi(\delta t)\left(\widetilde{\nu}_{i}+\bar{v}_{i}\gamma_{i}\right)}{1+\phi(\delta t)a\varphi_{i}\frac{I^{p}_{q}}{H^{p}}}I^{p}_{i}+\frac{\phi(\delta t)\left(\widetilde{\nu}_{i}+v_{i}\zeta_{i}\right)}{1+\phi(\delta t)a\varphi_{i}\frac{I^{p}_{q}}{H^{p}}}R^{p}_{i}&i=0,\;1,\;\cdots,\;n\\
S^{p+1}_{q}&~=~\frac{\phi(\delta t)\Gamma}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}+\frac{1-\phi(\delta t)\mu}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}S^{p}_{q}+\frac{\phi(\delta t)\delta}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}S_{r}^{p}&\,\\
S^{p+1}_{r}&~=~\frac{{\phi(\delta t)}^{2}(r_{suc}^{p}-r_{inf}^{p})\Gamma}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}+\frac{\phi(\delta t)(r_{suc}^{p}-r_{inf}^{p})\left(1-\phi(\delta t)\mu\right)}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}S^{p}_{q}\\
&~~~+~\frac{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p}-\delta-\mu)-{\phi(\delta t)}^{2}((\mu+\delta)d^{p}+\mu r_{suc}^{p}+\delta r_{inf}^{p})}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}S_{r}^{p}&\,\\
E^{p+1}_{i}&~~=~~u_{i}a\,\varphi_{i}\frac{I^{p}_{q}}{H^{p}}\phi(\delta t)S^{p+1}_{i}+(1-u_{i}\phi(\delta t))\left(\varepsilon_{i}+\nu_{i}\right)E^{p}_{i}&i=0,\;1,\;\cdots,\;n\,\\
E^{(1)p+1}_{r}&~=~\phi(\delta t)r_{inf}^{p}S^{p+1}_{q}+\left(1-\phi(\delta t)(\mu+\delta)\right)E^{(1)p}_{r}&\,\\
E^{(j)p+1}_{q}&~=~\frac{\phi(\delta t)\delta}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}E^{(j)p}_{r}+\frac{1-\phi(\delta t)\mu}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}E^{(j)p}_{q}&j=1,\;2,\;\cdots,\;l\,\\
E^{(j+1)p+1}_{r}&~=~\frac{{\phi(\delta t)}^{2}r_{suc}^{p}\delta}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}E^{(j)p}_{r}+\frac{\phi(\delta t)r_{suc}^{p}(1-\phi(\delta t)\mu)}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}E^{(j)p}_{q}\\
&~~~+~\left(1-\phi(\delta t)(\mu+\delta)\right)E^{(j+1)p}_{r}&\;j=1,\;2,\;\cdots,\;l\,\\
I^{p+1}_{i}&~=~\bar{u}_{i}a\varphi_{i}\frac{I^{p}_{q}}{H^{p}}\phi(\delta t)S^{p+1}_{i}+u_{i}\varepsilon_{i}\phi(\delta t)E^{p}_{i}+\left(1-\phi(\delta t)(\nu_{i}+\gamma_{i})\right)I^{p}_{i}&i=0,\;1,\;\cdots,\;n\,\\
R^{p+1}_{i}&~~=~~v_{i}\gamma_{i}\phi(\delta t)I^{p}_{i}+(1-v_{i}\left(\zeta_{i}+\nu_{i}\right)\phi(\delta t))R^{p}_{i}&i=0,\;1,\;\cdots,\;n\,\\
I^{p+1}_{q}&~=~\frac{\phi(\delta t)\delta}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}E^{(l+1)p}_{r}+\frac{1-\phi(\delta t)\mu}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}I^{p}_{q}+\frac{\phi(\delta t)\delta}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}I^{p}_{r}&\,\\
I^{p+1}_{r}&~=~\frac{{\phi(\delta t)}^{2}r_{suc}^{p}\delta}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}E^{(l+1)p}_{r}+\frac{\phi(\delta t)r_{suc}^{p}(1-\phi(\delta t)\mu)}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}I^{p}_{q}\\
&~~~+~\frac{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p}-\delta-\mu)-{\phi(\delta t)}^{2}((\mu+\delta)d^{p}+\mu r_{suc}^{p})}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}I^{p}_{r}\end{aligned}\right.

(77)

The system (
77
) can be written in shortened form as

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcr}\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1}-\mathbf{x}_{1}^{*}&=&\mathbf{A}_{S,\delta t}\left(\mathbf{x}^{p}\right)\,.\,\left(\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p}-\mathbf{x}_{1}^{*}\right)+\mathbf{A}_{S,\,I,\delta t}(\mathbf{x}^{p})\,.\,\mathbf{x}_{2}^{p}\\
\mathbf{x}^{p+1}_{2}&=&\mathbf{A}_{I,\delta t}\left(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}^{p+1}_{1}\right)\,.\,\mathbf{x}_{2}^{p}\end{array}\right.,

(78)

where the sub-matrices in (
78
) are defined as follows: First we have the
(n+2)\times(n+2)
diagonal matrix

\mathbf{A}_{S,h}\left(\mathbf{x}^{p}\right)=\mathrm{diag}\left(\mathbf{A}_{S_{H},h}\left(\mathbf{x}^{p}\right),\;\mathbf{A}_{S_{V},h}\left(\mathbf{x}^{p}\right)\right),

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{S_{H},h}\left(\mathbf{x}^{p}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{diag}\left(\frac{1-(\nu_{i}-\widetilde{\nu}_{i})\phi(h)}{1+a\phi(h)\varphi_{i}\frac{I^{p}_{q}}{H^{p}}}\right)_{1\leqslant i\leqslant n};\qquad\mathbf{A}_{S_{V},h}\left(\mathbf{x}^{p}\right)=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1-\phi(\delta t)\mu}{1+\phi(h)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}&\frac{\phi(h)\delta}{1+\phi(h)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}\cr\frac{\phi(h)(r_{suc}^{p}-r_{inf}^{p})(1-\phi(h)\mu)}{1+\phi(h)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}&\xi(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;h)\end{pmatrix};

\displaystyle\xi(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;h)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1+\phi(h)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p}-\delta-\mu)-{\phi(h)}^{2}((\mu+\delta)d^{p}+\mu r_{suc}^{p}+\delta r_{inf}^{p})}{1+\phi(h)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}.

The
(n+2)\times(3n+2l+3)
matrix
\mathbf{A}_{S,\,I,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})
may be written in block form as:

\mathbf{A}_{S,\,I,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{A}_{S,\,E_{H},h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})&\mathbf{0}_{n,\,2l+1}&\mathbf{A}_{S,\,I_{H},h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})&\mathbf{A}_{S,\,R_{H},h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})&\mathbf{0}_{n,\,2}\cr\mathbf{0}_{2,\,n}&\mathbf{0}_{2,\,2l+1}&\mathbf{0}_{2,\,n}&\mathbf{0}_{2,\,n}&\mathbf{0}_{2,\,2}\end{pmatrix},

where
\mathbf{A}_{S,\,E_{H},h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})
,
\mathbf{A}_{S,\,I_{H},h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})
and
\mathbf{A}_{S,\,R_{H},h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})
are respectively

\ \mathrm{diag}\begin{pmatrix}\dfrac{u_{i}\phi(h)\widetilde{\nu}_{i}}{1+\phi(h)a\varphi_{i}\frac{I^{p}_{q}}{H^{p}}}\end{pmatrix}_{1\leqslant i\leqslant n},\ \mathrm{diag}\begin{pmatrix}\dfrac{\phi(h)\left(\widetilde{\nu}_{i}+\bar{v}_{i}\gamma_{i}\right)}{1+\phi(h)a\varphi_{i}\frac{I^{p}_{q}}{H^{p}}}\end{pmatrix}_{1\leqslant i\leqslant n},\hbox{ and }\mathrm{diag}\begin{pmatrix}\dfrac{v_{i}\phi(h)\left(\widetilde{\nu}_{i}+\zeta_{i}\right)}{1+\phi(h)a\varphi_{i}\frac{I^{p}_{q}}{H^{p}}}\end{pmatrix}_{1\leqslant i\leqslant n}.

The matrix
\mathbf{A}_{I,\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})
may be written in block form as

\mathbf{A}_{I,\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{A}_{I_{E},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})&\mathbf{A}_{I_{I,\,E},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})\\
\mathbf{A}_{I_{E,\,I},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})&\mathbf{A}_{I_{I},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})\end{array}\right),

(79)

where the four block matrices may be described as follows:

The
(n+2l+1)\times(n+2l+1)
matrix
\mathbf{A}_{I_{E},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})
may be written as
\mathbf{A}_{I_{E},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})=\mathrm{diag}\left(\mathbf{A}_{I_{E_{H}},\,h},~\mathbf{A}_{I_{E_{V}},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})\right)
with

\mathbf{A}_{I_{E_{H}},\,h}=\mathrm{diag}((1-u_{i}\phi(h))\left(\varepsilon_{i}+\nu_{i}\right))_{1\leqslant i\leqslant n}

and
\mathbf{A}_{I_{E_{V}},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})
is a 3-banded sub-diagonal matrix whose diagonal and sub-diagonal elements are given by the vectors
\mathbf{d}_{0,\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})
,
\mathbf{d}_{-1,\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})
and
\mathbf{d}_{-2,\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})
respectively, defined by

\displaystyle\mathbf{d}_{0,\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})

\displaystyle=\Big(\underbrace{1-\phi(h)(\mu+\delta),\;\frac{1-\phi(h)\mu}{1+\phi(h)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})},\;\cdots,1-\phi(h)(\mu+\delta),\;\frac{1-\phi(h)\mu}{1+\phi(h)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}}_{2l\;\;components},\;1-\phi(h)(\mu+\delta)\Big),

\displaystyle\mathbf{d}_{-1,\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})

\displaystyle=\frac{\phi(h)}{1+\phi(h)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}{\left(\right.}\underbrace{\delta,\;r_{suc}^{p}(1-\phi(h)\mu),\;\cdots,\;\delta,\;r_{suc}^{p}(1-\phi(h)\mu)}_{2l\;\;components}{\left.\right.)},

\displaystyle\mathbf{d}_{-2,\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})

\displaystyle=\frac{\phi(h)^{2}r_{suc}^{p}}{1+\phi(h)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}{\left(\right.}\underbrace{\delta,\;0,\;\cdots,\;\delta,\;0}_{2(l-1)\;\;components},\;\delta).

The
(n+2l+1)\times 2(n+1)
matrix
\mathbf{A}_{I_{I,\,E},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})
that may be written in block form as

\mathbf{A}_{I_{I,\,E},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{0}&\mathbf{A}_{I_{I_{V},\,E_{H}},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})\cr\mathbf{A}_{I_{I_{H},\,E_{V}},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})&\mathbf{0}\end{pmatrix}

with blocks
\mathbf{A}_{I_{I_{V},\,E_{H}},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})
and
\mathbf{A}_{I_{I_{H},\,E_{V}},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})
given respectively by

\mathbf{A}_{I_{I_{V},\,E_{H}},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})=a\frac{\phi(h)}{H^{p}}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}m_{1}S_{1}^{p+1}&0\\
m_{2}S_{2}^{p+1}&0\\
\vdots&\vdots\\
m_{n}S_{n}^{p+1}&0\end{array}\right),\qquad\mathbf{A}_{I_{I_{H},\,E_{V}},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})=a\phi(h)\frac{S^{p+1}_{q}}{H^{p}}\begin{pmatrix}\xi_{1}\phi_{1}&\cdots&\xi_{n}\phi_{n}&\widetilde{\xi}_{1}\phi_{1}&\cdots&\widetilde{\xi}_{n}\phi_{n}\\
0&\cdots&0&0&\cdots&0\\
\vdots&\cdots&\vdots&\vdots&\cdots&\vdots\\
0&\cdots&0&0&\cdots&0\end{pmatrix}.

The
2n+2)\times(n+2l+1)
matrix
\mathbf{A}_{I_{E,\,I},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})
may be written in block form as

\mathbf{A}_{I_{E,\,I},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{A}_{I_{{E_{H}},\,{I_{H}}},\,h}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\mathbf{A}_{I_{{E_{V}},\,{I_{V}}},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})\end{pmatrix}

where the block matrix
\mathbf{A}_{I_{{E_{H}},\,{I_{H}}},\,h}
is the
n\times n
matrix

\mathbf{A}_{I_{{E_{H}},\,{I_{H}}},\,h}=\mathrm{diag}(u_{0}\phi(h)\varepsilon_{0},~\cdots,~u_{n}\phi(h)\varepsilon_{n})

and the block matrix
\mathbf{A}_{I_{{E_{V}},\,{I_{V}}},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})
is the
(n+2)\times(2l+1)
matrix that has only two non-zero entries; the
(1,2l+1)
entry is equal to

\frac{\phi(h)\delta}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}
, and the
(2,2l+1)
entry is equal to
\frac{{\phi(\delta t)}^{2}r_{suc}^{p}\delta}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}
.

The
(2n+2)\times(2n+2)
matrix

\mathbf{A}_{I_{I},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})
may be written in block form as:

\mathbf{A}_{I_{I},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{A}_{I_{I_{H}},\,h}&\mathbf{A}_{I_{I_{H},I_{V}},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})\\
\mathbf{0}&\mathbf{A}_{I_{I_{V}},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})\end{pmatrix}

where
the block matrix
\mathbf{A}_{I_{I_{H}},\,h}
is the two band diagonal matrix of order
2n\times 2n
with the principal diagonal constituted with the terms on the vectors

\mathbf{a}_{0}=\left((1-\phi(h)(\nu_{1}+\gamma_{1})),\;\cdots,\;(1-\phi(h)(\nu_{n}+\gamma_{n})),\;(1-\phi(h)v_{1}(\nu_{1}+\zeta_{1})),\;\cdots,\;(1-\phi(h)v_{n}(\nu_{n}+\zeta_{n}))\right)

and the
n^{th}
sub-diagonal constituted with terms of the vector
\mathbf{a}_{n}=\phi(h)\left(v_{1}\gamma_{1},\;\cdots,\;v_{n}\gamma_{n}\right)
and
\mathbf{A}_{I_{I_{H},I_{V}},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})
, that is
2n\times 2
is
\mathbf{A}_{I_{I_{H},I_{V}},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p},\;\mathbf{x}_{1}^{p+1})=\dfrac{a\phi(h)}{H^{p}}\begin{pmatrix}\bar{u}_{1}\varphi_{1}S_{1}^{p+1}&0\cr\vdots&\vdots\cr\bar{u}_{n}\varphi_{n}S_{n}^{p+1}&0\cr 0&0\cr\vdots&\vdots\cr 0&0\end{pmatrix}

The last
2\times 2
square block matrix
A_{I_{I_{V}},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})
is

A_{I_{I_{V}},\,h}(\mathbf{x}^{p})=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1-\phi(h)\mu}{1+\phi(h)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}&\frac{\phi(h)\delta}{1+\phi(h)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}\\
\frac{\phi(h)r_{suc}^{p}(1-\phi(h)\mu)}{1+\phi(h)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}&\frac{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p}-\delta-\mu)-{\phi(\delta t)}^{2}((\mu+\delta)d^{p}+\mu r_{suc}^{p})}{1+\phi(\delta t)(d^{p}+r_{suc}^{p})}\end{pmatrix}.
